### Player powers and the ceasefire (WoL #14, 2026-10-30)

A power is cast per player, not per unit: the button of any unit only spends the player's charge of
that power. Evidence: `rea/evidence/age3y-b1a1dab7/ceasefire14-bundle.json` (`ev_87a250ac…` for
FUN_006ED5F6, `ev_3ffa4dd0…` and `ev_b14fb3f1…` for the two strings), capstone listings, and memory
reads in game (`ceasefire14/powers.py`).

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Power type `CeaseFire` | 19h, registered at 0x526D3A (string 0xBD6344) | confirmed | Next to `GoodFortune` (18h) and `stun` (1Ah). Nothing in the exe compares the caster's proto with the Taj Mahal. The only Taj Mahal reads are the proto name `ypTajMahal` into `[db+5FC4h]` (0x5EF2D1), compared at 0x694A5B with other wonder age-up protos (UI), and the tech names `YPWonderIndianTajMahal…`. |
| `<alwaysaddtoplayer>` | powers.xml parser FUN_005F230D, 0x5F385C | confirmed | `or dword ptr [power+6Ch], 8` (0x5F3887). Its only reader is the grant below (0x6ED68D). |
| Grant at game start | FUN_006ED5F6, `bool thiscall(abilityMgr, player*)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (static and in game) | Run once per player from the game start loop (0x6AED32..0x6AED9C, through 0x8D017B). For each ability record: the power is granted when the record has no tech, or its tech is already active (0x94AEF4), or the power has flag 8. Grant = `0x8D43B1(power, rof, now + rof)` and `0x8D75F5(power, 1)`. A record whose tech becomes active later grants nothing, and no tech effect grants it then (`PowerBalloon`, given by HC cards, carries `alwaysaddtoplayer` for this reason). |
| Recharge list | `[player+3F0h]`, count `[player+3F4h]`, 12-byte entries `{power, rof ms, time ms}` | confirmed (memory) | One entry per power ID: a second record of the same power overwrites the ROF, so the last granted record sets the period of every caster (450 s for `ypPowerCeaseFire`, from the Taj Mahal records). After a cast, `time` = cast time + that ROF. |
| Charge list | `[player+3E4h]`, count `[player+3E8h]`, 12-byte entries `{power, charges, recharging}` | confirmed (memory) | Found by 0x8D4306 (`entry* thiscall(player, power)`, `ret 4`). Granted with 1 charge; a cast sets `{0, 1}` until the recharge ends. |
| Use a power | FUN_008D99D1, called from 0x8D9E0B and 0x8D9E6A | partly confirmed | Fails (0x8D9C20) when the player has no charge entry for the power or `charges` ≤ 0 (0x8D9A1D..0x8D9A30). In game the UI then prints string "This ability is not available" and nothing starts. |
| Ceasefire state | the treaty (no-rush) object; `[obj+4]` end time, `[obj+9]` one-minute warning shown, `[obj+11h]` 1 for a ceasefire | partly confirmed | Its update (cold chunk 0x6776A8..) picks "one minute to the end of the Ceasefire" (69388) or string 46616, and "Ceasefire over" (69391) or 46617, from `[obj+11h]`, with the alert `ui\alerts\treatyend.xml`; the timer text is 69387 (0x8C1671). Sharing the treaty object is the likely reason a ceasefire cast in a Treaty game ends the treaty early (inference). |

### Lifespan and construction (for `lifespanAfterConstruction`)

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Lifespan tick | FUN_00482BF6 | confirmed, in game | `void __thiscall(unit, float dt)`, `ret 4`. Hot part returns (0x482C20 `pop esi / pop ecx / ret 4`) unless `[game+B3Ah]` = 0 and state bit 3 is set; cold part 0x483EEC: dt ≤ 0 returns; less than dt left → expiry 0x4CBABC (state bit 11 set: only removes the corpse; else zeroes the countdown, clears bit 3 and queues the kill 0x4B4DC2); else `[sub+1Ch] -= dt`. Callers 0x483104 (full unit update), 0x51169E (light update). |
| Fade update | FUN_004829FA | confirmed | `float __thiscall(unit, float dt, bool commit)`, `ret 8`. `t = [sub+88h] − dt` into `[esp+10h]`, stored at 0x482A20 when commit; then bit 61 → 0x4E5F88, bit 4 (fade-in) → 0x6A1CE1, bit 5 → 0x4A174A, which returns while t > 0 and, when the alpha reaches 0, calls setLifespan(2e-6) (0x4CBB2E). (`docs/performance.md` calls this function "movement"; that label is wrong.) |
| setLifespan | FUN_0048DCB9 | confirmed | `void __thiscall(unit, float seconds, bool alphaFade)`, `ret 8`. `[sub+1Ch]` = seconds; > 0 → state bit 3 (`fcomp [0B84BE4h] / test ah,41h`, then `or byte ptr [[esi+4]],8` at 0x48DCDE; site `SetLifespanArm`); alphaFade → fade-out scheduled through 0x47B50C. Callers include unit init 0x498372 (with `[proto+ACh]` and flag `AlphaFadeLifespan`), the fade end 0x4CBB2E and the Lifespan tech effect 0xA005D9. `startingTC` reads bit 3 as "lifespan pending". |
| Schedule a fade-out | FUN_0047B50C | confirmed (body) | `__thiscall(unit, float duration, float delay)`: `[sub+84h]`, `[sub+88h]`, state bit 5 set, bit 61 cleared. Also used by the Death actions for the corpse fade. |
| Is built | FUN_0044DDB4 | confirmed, in game | `bool __thiscall(unit)`: `[unit+68h] == 0` or `[[unit+68h]+8] >= 1.0`. Changes EAX only; false exit 0x69F904. Earlier tables called it "alive"; it is "fully built". |
| Kill handler | FUN_004B4F70 | confirmed | Returns at once if state bit 11 is set; otherwise sets bit 11 (0x4B51F9) and clears bit 8 (0x4B520E). |
| Unit sub-object | `[unit+B4h]` | confirmed (as read above) | `+1Ch` lifespan left, `+84h` fade duration, `+88h` time before the fade, `+8Ch` alpha, `+90h` base LOS, `+94h` the resource inventory ("Resource inventories"), `+C8h` the modifier record (created with a unit's first stat modifier: `+4` max HP override, `+1Ch` LOS factor, `+24h` GatherRate, `+28h` AutoGatherRate, `+44h` army ID; the velocity too). State bits of `[unit+4]` used: 3 lifespan armed, 4 fade-in, 5 fade-out, 8 in the world, 11 dying, 12 not alive, 61 another fade. |
| `<Lifespan>` | `[proto+ACh]` (handler 0x5FCF4B) | confirmed (already in "Damage and poison") | Default 0. |

| What | Where | Status | Notes |

| Snare trigger | FUN_0048FA07 → cold chunk 0x4DD1DE | confirmed | Tactics action flag bit 11 (`<SpeedBoost>`) → 0x4B3E31; bit 12 (`<TargetSpeedBoost>`) → `0x4DD22D(target, 0, 0.65f, -1f, -1f, 0, 2, 3000, -1, -1, 1)` when the target can move. |
| Apply / create a modifier | 0x4DD22D → 0x4B3D9E; init 0x4B3D44 | confirmed, in game | `int __thiscall(unit, stat, float value, -1f, -1f, 0, operation, int ms, -1, -1, exclusive)`, `ret 28h`; −1 without an action manager `[unit+58h]` (0x69FDBD); allocates the modifier record `[[unit+B4h]+C8h]` when missing (0x4AAC67). `damageOverTime`'s `AttachSnare` calls it with the data's factor (site `SnareApply`; a Hussar read 3.38 = 6.75 × 0.5 through slot 34h). Unit action type 2Ch: `[+64h]` stat (0 = velocity), `[+68h]` value, `[+78h]` operation (0 set, 1 add, 2 multiply), `[+88h]` ms; flag 15h when timed, 14h when not; flag 16h = exclusive. Only the snare is both timed and exclusive (`<SpeedBoost>` is timed, not exclusive; the auras pass 0 ms). Auras (FUN_009D033E), powers (0x8EE18E) and a second applier (0x9D7696) use the same call (scoping; "Building work rate auras"). |
| Action lists | `[actionManager+14h]`, `{count +0, data +8}`; children at `[action+40h]`; type byte `[+60h]`; flag bitset `{[+30h] bytes, [+34h] count}` | confirmed | Walked depth first, parent first, by the collector 0x49F1A0 / 0x49F397 / 0x49F34B. `src/modifiers.h` repeats that walk, read-only, for `snareOnlyHitUnit` and `buildingWorkRateAuras`. |
| Velocity recompute | FUN_0049F1BA, the one call of 0x4B3C48 at 0x49F26A | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | The list 0xCF0718 ({data, count, capacity}) is built by the collector call 0x49F251 for each recompute, oldest modifier first, so stock's first exclusive snare is the oldest (in game: a 0.8 first hides a 0.3 after it, speed 3.20). `snareValues strongest` swaps the slowest into the first snare's place there. Add 0x48D975: appends unless its second argument is set. |
| Velocity modifier sum | 0x4B3C48 / 0x4B3C7D (cold 0x4E2A8E, 0x6A88D6) | confirmed | Starts from MaxVelocity `[proto+80h]`; applies each stat-0 modifier in order, rounded by `fstp`; stops after the first with flag 16h or whose tactics action has `<ModifyExclusive>` (bit 33). Stored in the record `[[unit+B4h]+C8h]` only if a modifier matched. The sum itself, 0x4B3C7D, serves every stat: `bool thiscall(unused ECX, float* v, {data +0, count +4}* list, int stat)`, `ret 0Ch`, true when a modifier of `stat` matched; no null check of the entries. |
| Unit max velocity | 0x487E27 (unit vtable slot 34h; cold 0x488612) | confirmed, in game | The record when it is above −1e-6, else MaxVelocity; times the cfg float `MovementMod` (0x44734C on 0xC66500 with the ID at `[[0xCEFFA0]]`; 1.0 when unset). |
| Group recompute | FUN_004FD289 | confirmed, in game | `[group+7Ch]` (pace) = the minimum of the members' `vtbl[34h]()` (0x4FD3FD..0x4FD411, store 0x4FD53A). Members `[group+98h]`, count `[group+9Ch]`. Triggered by group bit 13, set by 0x49F3D0 when a member's velocity changes. |
| Group speed | 0x4FAA5D | confirmed | Target speed = `[group+88h]` × pace; × 0.8333 when bit 7 ("a member is behind") is set (0x4FAAA5). |
| Straggler bit | 0x4FA17D (member step FUN_004FA03B), 0x6AA514 and 0x6AA56A (own-path branch of the member loop FUN_004F9E05) | confirmed, in game | Set when a member is more than 2.0 from its formation slot (0x4F9B69, scoping for the value). The member loop clears bit 7 first and looks units up by ID inline (0x4F9EB7). |
| Member speed | 0x4F9BCB | confirmed | `min(v·[group+88h], max(v, [group+84h]))`: a member never walks faster than its own velocity v. |

### Civ first town center (for `civFirstTC`)

The ranges the feature relies on are the `civFirstTC` sites of `tools/gen_sites.py`, whose
comments hold the disassembly they were read from; FirstTC itself is in "Starting town center"
above, Player by ID and Unit by ID in "Home city gather point". "In game" means the single-player
tests of 2026-10-29 ([modding-guide.md](modding-guide.md#civfirsttc)).

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| civs.xml parser | FUN_0052A590 | confirmed (the `<civ>` child loop around 0x52BAFA..0x52BB19 and 0x52B43D), in game | ESI = the child node, EBX = database, `[esp+10h]` = the civ being read. Each child tag is compared with the known names through 0x443C10; the last compare is `call 443C10h / test eax,eax / je 65A549h` at 0x52BAFA. A tag that matches none reaches 0x52BB07 (`xor edi,edi / cmp [ebx+80h],edi`), then `jle 52B43Dh / jmp 52C066h` (the resource names, then a silent skip), so stock ignores an unknown tag such as `<firstTC>`. Every handled tag continues at 0x52B43D (`mov eax,[esp+80h] / mov ebp,[esp+0B0h] / add eax,1 / cmp eax,[ebp+0Ch]`, the next child; reads only the stack). Sites `CivTagUnknown`, `CivTagNextNode`. In game, `<firstTC>` was read and logged. |
| XML node | the node in ESI | confirmed (the parser's compare chain), in game | Element name `[[node+4]+18h][[node+18h]]` (wide; the name table's count is `[[node+4]+1Ch]`), text `[node+14h]` (wide). |
| Civ object | `[civ+4]` name (`char*`), `[civ+8]` civ ID | confirmed, in game | The civ ID is the index in `[db+24h]` (see 0x58E71F). |
| Civ by ID | 0x405C2E | confirmed | `Civ* __thiscall(database, int id)`, `ret 4`: `[[db+24h]+4·id]` for 0 ≤ id < `[db+28h]`, else 0. Site `CivById`. |
| Player's civ ID | 0x4539D3 | confirmed | `int __thiscall(player)`: `[[player+20h]]` when `[player+24h]` > 0, else −1 (cold path). Site `CivOfPlayer`. |
| Proto by name / by ID | 0x5A2346 / 0x45316E | confirmed | 0x5A2346 (Functions table) scans `[db+54h]` (count `[db+58h]`) comparing `[proto+10h]` without case; −1 if none. 0x45316E: `Proto* __thiscall(database, int id)` = `[[db+54h]+4·id]`, `ret 4`. Sites `ProtoByName`, `ProtoById`. |

Stock `TownCenter` has it, `Market` does not. Other flags `TownCenter` has and `Market` lacks (stock `protoy`): `ColonyPlacementCenter`, `HasGatherPoint`, `AllowAutoGarrison`, `ShowGarrisonButton`; what they do was not traced. |

The water shipment point does not belong here: 0x855D71 reads `[hc+190h]`, then `[db+6028h]`, `HomeCityWaterSpawnFlag` (0x855E0B), and never `[db+5E08h]`. Read from the stock exe with capstone (REA was in use by another agent); the ranges are the `scenarioCivs` sites of
`tools/gen_sites.py`. "In game" means the single-player tests of 2026-11-22 (`modding-guide.md`, "scenarioCivs").

Creates civ 0 `Nature` (0x52A5C1), loads the file into a stack XML reader `[esp+100h]` (0x52A607..0x52A625), walks the root's elements (0x52A692..0x52B47A), then sets the database's other values, marks the native civs (0x52B54F..0x52B581) and destroys the reader (0x52B588). |

Releases a document the reader holds first (`cmp byte [esi+1864h],0 / call 598E2Bh`, 0x599A92). The root is the reader's `+8` (`[esp+108h]` in the loader). A loose `data\` file overrides the archive; an XML that is not well formed returns false with no dialog [O]. Site `XmlReaderLoad`. |
| End of the root walk | 0x52B485 (`push 0BCEE80h`) | confirmed (`cs`), in game | Reached from 0x52B480 (after the last element) and from the `jle` at 0x52A68C (a root without elements); nothing after it reads the reader's document. Callers 0x52A5C1 (Nature) and 0x52A707 (each `<civ>`). Site `ScenarioCivsAppend`. |
| Civ flags | `[civ+128h]` (pointer to a bitset, bit count `[civ+12Ch]`) | confirmed (`cs`), in game | Bit 0 `Visible` (handler 0x52D302), bit 1 `VisibleInEditor` (handler 0x52BE78), bit 2 set at 0x52CC1B by another tag. Nature's bits 0 and 1 are cleared at 0x52A5EF. Sites `CivFlagVisible`, `CivFlagEditor`. |

Which screens these three fill is inferred [I]. |

### Resource inventories (for `transformInventory`)

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Proto resource object | `[proto+E0h]` | confirmed (0x5F8643, 0x67DE1C) | 14h B, created at parse time on the first resource tag (0x5F811B, scoping): `+0` `InitialResource` list (handler 0x5F8665 → 0x5F8643, which calls the list setter on `[proto+E0h]`), `+8` `CarryCapacity` list (0x5FB65F → 0x5FB63A, scoping), `+10h` `GathererLimit` byte (default FFh, scoping). Null for a proto with no resource tag (e.g. `Musketeer`). |
| Resource list (ResList) | 8 B | confirmed | `+0` array of `[0xC6DF38]` floats, entry r XORed with `[0xC6DF14 + 4r]`; `+4` the total XORed with `[0xC6DF10]`. The keys are in `.data` (8 non-zero dwords at 0xC6DF14..0xC6DF33); the count is set at runtime. |
| ResList get | 0x44EFFF | confirmed | `float thiscall(list, int r)`, `ret 4`; 0 out of range or without an array. |
| ResList set | 0x58EF83 | confirmed | `thiscall(list, int r, float v)`, `ret 8`; recomputes the total. |
| ResList total recompute | 0x58EF02 | confirmed | `thiscall(list)`; sums the decoded entries (x87) and stores the encoded total. Site `ResListTotal`. |
| ResList copy | 0x480A73 | confirmed | `thiscall(dst, src)`, `ret 4`, returns dst; copies the raw encoded entries and the total, only when both arrays exist. Site `ResListCopy`. |
| ResList scale | 0x480B94 | confirmed | `thiscall(list, float f)`, `ret 4`; multiplies each decoded entry (x87), then 0x58EF02. Site `ResListScale`. |
| ResList fill / setTotal | 0x4E2617 / 0x447191 | confirmed | `thiscall(list, float v)`, `ret 4`: every entry = v, total = count × v / the total only. |
| Resource names | 0x5381E0 | confirmed; in game for Food | Registers Gold 0, Wood 1, Food 2, Fame 3, SkillPoints 4, XP 5, Ships 6, Trade 7 in a name table at `[obj+7Ch]`. That these IDs are the list indices was seen in game for Food (a crate logged `{2: 100}` showed 100 food), for Gold (a trigger's 1234 gold) and XP (below); for the others it is an inference (8 names, 8 keys). |
| Player resources | `[player+230h]`, a ResList embedded in the player (its array pointer at +230h) | in game (2026-10-29) | Found by a memory scan after a trigger granted 1234 gold: entry 0 = 1234. A second list at `+240h` read the same values (its role is not known). Entry 5 (XP) rose by 4 about every 1.4 s of game time in a scenario with no other source (a steady XP trickle), and by the unit's `<Bounty>` on a kill (+10 for a musketeer, +20 for a Hussar), including a kill by a `damageOverTime` tick. |
| Skill points total | 0x5011C6 | confirmed (`cs`), in game (2026-11-23) | `int thiscall(player)`: int(resource 4) + the home city's skill points `[[player+84h]+1F4h]` (none without a home city). Read instead of the plain entry for resource 4 by the getter 0x86B616 (which also returns 0x47C83C, the total XP, for resource 5), the KB copy loop at 0x86D8BD and the code at 0x8767CA (`cmp reg,4`). The UI reads it too: a `{resource(skillpoints)}` label showed the home city's unspent skill points (1, 9) before anything was gathered, and `{resource(xp)}` the home city's XP plus the game's. Its six callers (capstone, 2026-11-24): the KB getter behind `kbResourceGet` 0x86B637, the KB copy loops 0x86D8C5 and 0x8767D2, the `{resource(name)}` data ref handler 0x63C8C6 (reached from 0x453EA9), `trPlayerResourceCount` 0x988538 (tail jump 0x98859E) and the per-player statistics snapshot 0x501211 (loop 0x500E2C, every resource). `faithResource` makes its `je 501209h` (0x501201) a `jmp`: every one of them then reads int(resource 4) alone. The home city purchase of a card spends `[HC+1F4h]` directly (0x857DB2: needs ≥ 1, decrements), never through this function. |
| Home city bank | FUN_00852924 (`thiscall(homecity)`, `[HC+8]` = player) | confirmed (REA `ev_b5972f48…`) | Adds resource 5 to the home city's XP `[HC+1F0h]` (capped in one branch at 30000 over a base) and resource 4 to its skill points `[HC+1F4h]`, then sets both resources to 0 through 0x49A8C5. Callers 0x70742A, 0x862C36 (then writes the home city, 0x859294), 0x865518, 0x980180. Resource 4 gathered in a game would become home city skill points. |
| Skill point seed | 0x6AEEDC, 0x6AF052 in FUN_006AED5E | confirmed (REA `ev_aaeb1a66…`) | At game setup, for players with a home city: `[HC+54h]` = level, resource 4 = level + 15 (0x49A8C5), `[HC+1F0h]` reset, resource 5 = 0.  Only for AI players (the loop at 0x6AED32 averages the levels of the players with `[player+B4h]` = 0, then seeds those with `[player+B4h]` ≠ 0), and then 0x85A63E resets the home city (skill points `[HC+1F4h]` = level − 1, 0x85A46E). **It is the AI's card budget** (in game 2026-11-24): an AI's resource 4 went 20 → 6 in the first 20 s of a stock skirmish while its `[HC+1F4h]` stayed 9; the XS rule `buyCards` reads it as `kbResourceGet(cResourceSkillPoints)` = resource 4 + `[HC+1F4h]`. |

Without the building's `<CarryCapacity>` the villagers walk to it and stand idle. |

A new anim name (`Pray`) needs no `animtypes.xml` entry: it played with the stock list. |

### Training near an enemy Town Center (for `noTrainNearEnemyTC`)

FirstTC and the two rule evaluators are in "Starting town center" above; Player by ID (0x44CB21)
and Unit by ID (0x44CC6D) in "Home city gather point".

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Train update | FUN_009EA28B (vtable 0xB98388 slot 3Ch) | confirmed (0x9EA28B..0x9EA4D6), in game | `void __thiscall(action)`. ESI = the action; `[action+10h]` the trainer, EDI = `[trainer+44h]` its player (0x9EA2B2, site `TrainUpdateTrainer`); `[action+61h]` state (14h = training); `[action+70h]` queue entries of 14h B, `[action+74h]` count. Site `TrainRateScale` covers 0x9EA424..0x9EA436. The meaning of `[world+334h]` is not known. |

The update completes the unit (setState 0Ah, 0x9EA4CC) when `[+6Ch]` ≥ `[0B856BCh]` = 1.0. In game, a rate of 0.0 held the progress. |
| isEnemy | 0x484D2D | confirmed | `bool __thiscall(player, id)`: `[player+1D4h+4·id] == 2` for 0 ≤ id < 10h, else false; `ret 4`. isAlly 0x4707EC: the same slot is 0 or 1. Site `PlayerIsEnemy`. |

That one asks slot 90h, 0x45EE55: when it is false, slot 108h, 0x4A0ED5, returns the distance from the point to the unit's obstruction outline (`[vtbl+1Ch]` → the obstruction, its shape at +10h; 0x485E26), 0 inside it; when it is true, slot F4h, 0x485DBC(from, to, 1), returns the centre distance in x and z minus `[vtbl+24h]` (0x48272C: the larger of `[proto+88h]` and `[proto+8Ch]`, read as the obstruction radii) × √2 (0xBA1384), at least 0 (0x485EDA). In game (`noTrainNearEnemyTC log`), from a Daimyo's centre: a Town Center (radii 4 × 4) 38 m away in x → 34.1 m, and 10.4 m → 6.4 m; a Barracks (4 × 4) 24 m in x and 4 in z → 20.0 m; a Stable (3 × 5) at the same place → 21.0 m. So for these buildings the distance is to the edge of the footprint. For a Hussar (a unit that can move) 18.4 m between centres gave 17.3 m, the radius branch. |
| Can move | 0x45EE55 (unit vtable slot 90h) | confirmed (head) | `bool __thiscall(unit)`, the stock snare's "target can move" test (0x4DD1EE, before 0x4DD22D) and the distance call's choice between radius and outline: false when bit 4 of the proto's flag bitset is set (`[[proto+50h]] & 10h`, with `[proto+54h]` > 4); otherwise it compares `[proto+80h]` with 1e-6 (0xB9FB64; the rest not read). The Town Center's first flag byte was 0xBE in game (bit 4 set). Which protos have bit 4 is not mapped. |
| Point to outline | 0x485E35 / 0x485E26 | confirmed (static) | 0x485E35: squared distance from a point to a 4-corner outline: 0 when 0x4858F0 finds the point inside, else the minimum of 0x423104 (point to segment) over the four edges. 0x485E26 returns its square root. |

A box query (point ± radius in x and z) through 0x462DBA into the obstruction manager's scratch list `[[world+260h]+4ECh]` (count `+4F0h`); with `precise` the entries whose distance to the point (`[vtbl+B8h]`) is at most the radius are appended to `out` = {data, count, capacity}, whose count is reset first when the capacity is ≥ 0; it grows through 0x401113, and the placement rule frees it with 0x4014C3. Callers: the rule's type branch (0x8CEA86, site `TypeRuleQuery` 0x8CEA69..0x8CEA8B, point `[esp+30h]`, radius `[esp+10h]`), the aura scan 0x9CFF9A, `noTrainNearEnemyTC near` (site `UnitsNearPoint`). |

Returns −1 when `[game+0B39h]` ≠ 0 or `[game+0B3Bh]` = 0, when the unit ID is negative or the text is empty (0x4019FF); else it fills a 4Ch-byte entry (text copy 0x40196F, time1, time2, unit ID, motion, colour ARGB, scale, `flag` as bit 2 of its bitset 0x80214D, `n`) and appends it through 0x4D5450 to the manager's array (data `[manager]`, count `[manager+4]`), returning its index. Stock callers: the dodge 0x6A2604 (time1/time2 from the cfg, motion {0, 0.1, 0}), 0x9C039E (2000, 1000, {0, 0.1, 0}), and 0x6A2AEE, 0x6A2B84, 0x9CF787 (not read); colour `[player+0C8h]` (ARGB: 0xFF2D2DF5 blue, 0xFFD22828 red in game). The second motion component makes the text rise: at 0.1 it left the screen in about 0.3 s in the test bed; at 0 it stayed over the unit (`noTrainNearEnemyTC`). Site `FloatingTextAdd`. |

### Gathering and the stat panel (for `showGatherRate`)

The proto flag test (0x45EE02, flag 9Bh), the raw bit test (0x44CAC4, indices 37h / 58h), the
world's `+318h`, the player's `+338h` / `+37Ch` and the BString functions are in the tables above;
the Gathering dance (FUN_004ECD39) in "Dances".

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gather tick (BUnitGatherAction::collectStuff_2, WoL's symbol) | FUN_004ECD87 | confirmed, in game | `thiscall(action, float elapsed)`, `ret 4`. Frame: `sub esp,1Ch`, push esi/edi, push ebp (0x4ECDCF), push ebx (0x4ECE1F): elapsed at `[esp+30h]`, amount `[esp+14h]`, the target's holding `[esp+1Ch]`, the unit of ID `[action+64h]` at `[esp+24h]`, target `[esp+28h]`. Returns at once while byte `[game+B3Ah]` is set. R = ((cur − base) + k·base) · `[world+318h]` · dance (0x4ECFDA..0x4ED02F; k = `[[[unit+B4h]+C8h]+24h]` above 1e-6, else 1, cold 0x69FE53); R ≤ 1e-6 ends the action (0x6A8AEA); at 0x4ED044 ST0 = R, the only x87 value (site `GatherTickRate`). Then × elapsed, clamped to the target's holding, × `[player+37Ch]` (0x4ED13A), 0 for an `AbstractShrine` target (0x4ED14B) or `[player+338h]` ≠ 0 (0x4ED1AB), proto-action flag 4 → the gatherer's own inventory (0x4ED15D), add 0x49A98A (0x4ED1EE); resource 5 (XP, inferred) also goes through 0x9C02C0 (0x4ED1F3). Only caller 0x4ECCD8. In game, a Settler on a berry bush recorded 0.670/s. |
| doAnimationLooped (WoL's symbol) | 0x4ECC88, Gather action vtable 0xB97880 (slot 0xB978E8) | confirmed (head) | Only while byte `[action+61h]` = 5: adds `[world+104h]` to `[action+88h]`, then calls collectStuff_2 with elapsed = 0x4939BD(gatherer) (the animation loop's length), or collectStuff 0x9DD7B4 when action bit 22 is set (0x4ECCB1, cold 0x6A8CED; gathering into the unit's own inventory, scoping). |
| Gather action fields | — | confirmed (reads in 0x4ECD87) | `+4` proto action, `+10h` gatherer unit, `+14h` target unit ID, `+61h` state byte (5 = gathering), `+64h` a unit ID (looked up at 0x4ECDAB), `+70h` resource index, `+88h` animation time accumulator. |
| Current action | `[[unit+58h]+24h]` | scoping | Set by the action manager's add 0x494894 (verifier). Actions come from a per-type pool (0x48D927, `[world+1A4h]`). |
| Stat panel fill | FUN_004D70ED (EBP frame; hot part to ~0x4D82B4, cold part 0x698A00..0x69C800) | confirmed (0x4D76DB..0x4D783F, 0x69AF6D..0x69B187), in game | `[ebp+8]` = selection (`+14h` count; byte `+21h` must be 0 for the AutoGather row, meaning unknown); `[esp+2Ch]` = next row (2 after HP, 0x4D76E0); `[esp+38h]` = first selected unit; `[esp+50h]` = the panel. AutoGather gate 0x4D76FF..0x4D782E (site `StatPanelAutoGatherGate`); join point 0x4D7834 (site `StatPanelAfterAutoGather`; 16 branches land on it, scoping; none inside 0x4D7835..0x4D783A). |
| Minimized interface | stat panel fill 0x4D721B..0x4D7261; unit panel 0x69922D..0x699456 | confirmed, in game | Profile option 49h `optionminimizedui` on: the stat panel fill returns at 0x4D7261 (`je 4D81A1h`). The unit panel (the minimized block of FUN_004C7E52, see "Unit panels" below; not 0x4911D1, which is FUN_004911CE, REA `ev_5245d596…`; `[esp+50h]` = panel, `[esp+54h]` = unit) then writes the AutoGather rate itself, in `[panel+33Ch]` `unitResourceRateLabel` and `[panel+340h]` `unitResourceRateValue` (bound at 0x9BB0D4, 0x9BB101). Only the `uiunitselectionmin*.xml` files have these gadgets. `showGatherRate` hooks the join point 0x699456. |
| Stat panel rows | `[panel+174h+i*4]` labels, `[panel+198h+i*4]` values, i = 0..8 | scoping (bind 0x9BBAD1) | `StackLabel%d` / `StackValue%d` of `uiunitstatpanel3`; the row writer resolves them with 0x4C519C. |
| Row writer | 0x69AF7C..0x69AFDF | confirmed | Input EBX = row; ESI = label gadget, EDI = value gadget; a missing gadget jumps to 0x4D7834 (0x69AFD2, 0x69AFDA). Site `StatPanelRowWriter`. |
| Row after HP; Group Health row | 0x4D76DB; 0x69A9D3..0x69AB0F | confirmed, in game | `mov edi,2 / mov [esp+2Ch],edi`: the next row after the HP row (site `OverhealStatRow`, the `overhealHitpointBars stat` hook). Group Health writes row EDI: gadgets through 0x4C519C(`{handle, gadget}`, handle) and 0x40431B; label `L"Group Health"` (0xC36838, a literal) with style 0D6F5D0h and value with style 0C6657Ch (vtable +84h), text +80h, +54h; help texts `[gadget+68h]` set to `L""` (0x401A12, 0x40196F); then `add edi,1 / mov [esp+2Ch],edi`. Sites `GroupHealthRow`, `GadgetRef`, `BStringFromWide`, `BStringSet`. |
| BString from text / assign | 0x401A12 / 0x40196F (0x4013B0) | confirmed | `thiscall(BString*, const wchar_t*)` (clears the BString first) / `thiscall(BString* dst, const BString* src)`; 0x4013B0 assigns `(text, -1, -1)` to an existing BString. Free 0x4010CA. |
| Profile game options | `<Setting Name="option…">` of `NewProfile3.xml`, read through 0x402CE9(id, out) | confirmed (one) | The ID is the setting's index in the profile's `GameOptions` list: 49h = `optionminimizedui` (0x4C8233 for the portrait path, 0x661E03 when the panel is built, which then loads `uiunitselectionmin.xml`). |
| AutoGather resource loop | 0x69AFE0..0x69B028 | confirmed | EBX = 0..`[db+80h]`−1, rate = 0x49EBE4(`[esp+0Ch]` = the AutoGather action, EBX) stored with `fst [esp+1Ch]`; the first rate above 1e-6 goes to 0x69B02A; none: 0x4D7834. Site `StatPanelAgLoop`. |
| Row formatter | 0x69B02A..0x69B186 | confirmed, in game | Label `%s (<icon="(32)(%S)">):` (0xC36580) from 0x60782A / 0x58EA52 and the icon 0x706EFB; value `%.2f/s` (0xC36554), or `%d/s` (0xC36548, chop) when rate − floor(rate) ≤ 1e-6 (0x58F05D, 0xBA20B4), which writes `[esp+0Ch]` (0x69B0EF, 0x69B0FB); `add [esp+2Ch],1`; `jmp 4D7834h`. Site `StatPanelRowFormat`. In game it wrote `Alim. (icon): 0.67/s`. |
| Resource name | 0x60782A | confirmed | `int thiscall(db, resource)`, `ret 4`: the string ID `[[db+180h]+res*4]`, count `[db+184h]`; −1 out of range. Food is resource 2 (in game: `Alim.`, resource 2). Site `ResourceNameId`. |
| BString assign (forwarder) | 0x443BB8 | confirmed | `void thiscall(BString* this, const BString* src, int, int)`: forwards `[src]` to 0x4013B0 (assign from `wchar_t*`; −1, −1 = the whole string). Used by the string providers (0x6F5931). Site `BStringAssign`. |
| Data-ref registration | FUN_005CFA7C (ctor of the `{}` parser, vtable 0xB9EA48) | scoping (table), confirmed (0x5D2D38) | Entries `{id, type 1 float / 2 int / 3 string / 4 color, name, provider}`. Ids 19h / 1Ah share `mov ebx,5FDE49h` at 0x5D06E7 (imm32 0x5D06E8); id 68h `mov dword ptr [esp+2Ch],5FDE49h` at 0x5D2D38 (imm32 0x5D2D3C, site `GatherRateStringRef`). Ten more refs use the same stub. |
| Data-ref stub | 0x5FDE49 | confirmed | `xor al,al / ret 4` (WoL's symbol table calls it BCore::textureChanged: a folded stub; four direct callers elsewhere). Site `DataRefStub`. |
| Data-ref evaluators | vtable 0xB9EA48: +4 0xAB9544 (color), +8 0x44A25A (string), +0Ch 0xAB94A2 (int), +10h 0xAB9400 (float) | confirmed | `thiscall(this, id, arg, out*)`, `ret 0Ch`. The typed ones call `[entry+0Ch]` only when the registered type is theirs; the string one dispatches on it (jump table 0x44A40C: 1 → 0x44A31D, 2 → 0x44A2C8, 3 → 0x44A635, 4 → 0x44A391). Each sets `Value.type` to the registered type, `[0C6DF7Ch]` = arg during the call (0 after), and checks the vfunc `[[0C66244h]]+4Ch` first. Type 3: Value `{3, BString 0}`, copied out with 0x40196F on true (site `DataRefStringEval`). |
| Data refs' current unit | `[[[0C67310h]+920h]+13Ch]` | confirmed (0x6F49B1) | A unit ID, looked up in `[[0C66234h]+13Ch]` (ID & 3FFFFh, stored ID must match). Other providers use other notions (0x6F5931: 0x4051B6 / 0x930041). Site `CurrentUnitRef`. |

### Unit panels: the big stat panel and the minimized selection panel (WoL #67, research, 2026-11-17)

Read with REA (Ghidra 12.1.4, snapshot `age3y-b1a1dab7.json`, bundle `rea/evidence/age3y-b1a1dab7/minui-20261117.json`) and
with capstone where Ghidra splits the functions (it files FUN_004D70ED as 0x4D70ED..0x4D7124 only, `ev_c6aff8e0…`). The rows
seen in game are in [unit-panel-catalog.md](unit-panel-catalog.md), with the screenshots.

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Two panel objects | selection panel (`uiunitselection.xml` / `uiunitselectionmin.xml`, 0xC04534 / 0xC0455C); stat panel (`uiunitstatpanel3.xml` / `uiunitstatpanel3min.xml`, 0xC043A4 / 0xC043D0) | confirmed (REA `ev_a0ca8d76…`) | The minimized UI swaps both files. Each panel binds its own gadgets by name. |
| Selection panel bind | 0x9BAD00..0x9BB194 | confirmed (capstone) | Through 0xAB7C2F: `+2E4h` unitPortrait, `+2E8h` unitTypeName, `+2ECh` unitPlayerName, `+2F0h`/`+2F4h` unitHitPointsLabel/Value, `+2F8h`/`+2FCh` unitResistsLabel/Value, `+300h`/`+304h` unitResourceLabel/Value, `+308h` unitPortraitCount, `+30Ch` its background, `+310h`/`+314h`/`+318h` health bars, `+31Ch` unitHelpButton, `+320h` buttonArea, `+324h` StackButtonBigBackground, `+328h` stackQueue, `+32Ch` tabCommand, `+330h` tabStats, `+334h` showCommands, `+338h` showStats, `+33Ch`/`+340h` unitResourceRateLabel/Value, `+344h + i*4` `unitselectionpanel-StackLabel%d` and `+350h + i*4` `StackValue%d`, i = 0..2 (loop `cmp edi,3` at 0x9BB18F); `byte [+13Ch]` = 1 when done. A missing gadget gets the "none" handle `[0C672C0h]`. |
| Stat panel bind | 0x9BBAFC..0x9BBC1F | confirmed (capstone) | `+174h + i*4` `unitstatpanel3-StackLabel%d`, `+198h + i*4` `StackValue%d`, i = 0..8 (`cmp edi,9` at 0x9BBB5F); `+164h` minimize, `+160h` help, `+168h` helpText, `+16Ch` back. |
| Selection panel fill | FUN_004C7E52 (hot 0x4C7E52..0x4C83F5, REA `ev_47f3d728…`; cold 0x6986C0..0x69A61F) | confirmed (capstone) | Name into `+2E8h` (0x4C80D0, 0x4702DC), portrait count, health bars by `[group+1Ch]` (0x4C82EA), then shows portrait, type name, player name, help button (0x447F7B). At 0x4C8235 it reads profile option 49h; set: `je 6987D7h` (0x4C827B), the **minimized stats block**, which comes back at 0x4C8281 (`jmp` at 0x69A60F). |
| Pie chart filling | `[gadget+130h]`, 20h bytes; material `[gadget+12Ch]` | confirmed (REA `ev_18d1be98…` FUN_00629A7A, in game 2026-11-26) | The overlay of a train button. Parsed from a gadget's `<pieChartFilling color rotation fill texture>` child by the base layout parser FUN_00629A7A (0x62A8C7): fraction `+0`, colour `+4` (ARGB, 0x404DF6), texture `+8` (-1 none), `+0Ch` 1.0, rotation `+1Ch` (1 cw), fill flag `+1Dh`, on `+1Eh`; the material `defaultcolored_alphablend` with diffuse = the texture. Seen in game: the covered part is the part not done, the uncovered wedge grows clockwise from 12 o'clock; no texture covers the whole square. Used by `progresspie.*`. |
| Pie fill slot | vtable +6Ch of every gadget class = 0xAB6C63 | confirmed (REA `ev_0ba07671…`, `ev_e59ee723…` 170 vtables) | `thiscall(gadget, float)`, `ret 4`: makes the record when missing (88FFFFFFh, texture -1, cw, fill false, on), then the fraction clamped to [0, 1] (0xB84BE4, 0xB856BC). |
| Pie on / off | 0x4C51FA (cold 0x6D206D) | confirmed (REA `ev_0ef63318…`, in game) | `thiscall(gadget, bool)`, `ret 4`: writes `+1Eh`; on with no record first calls vtable +6Ch(0). 30 callers (command panel 0x69C37E.., stack buttons 0x9B8ACC.., special powers 0x70B35B). |

| Min block: player name | 0x6987D7..0x698A82 | confirmed (capstone) | `[panel+2ECh]`: `%s (%s)`, the player's name and civ (0x4D83F9), or `%s`; colour from the player. |
| Min block: HP | 0x698A82..0x698BE2 | confirmed, in game | `[panel+2F0h]` = string 24320 `Hitpoints:`, `[panel+2F4h]` = `%d/%d` (HP `[unit+64h]`, max 0x480408). Site `OverhealPanelMax` is 0x698B35 here. |

Label string 35758 `Resists:`, value `%.2f %s` / `%d %s` with the damage type icon of `[proto+1D3h]` (0 hand `[[game+920h]+1CCh]`, 1 ranged `+1D0h`, 2 siege `+1D4h`), help text string 42223. A building has no `Unit` type: the gadget stays hidden, and so does every gadget nested in it (WoL's speed). |

Label `%s (<icon="(32)(%S)">):` (0x60782A, 0x706EFB); value `%d/%d` (0x4E5979 amount, 0x494503 total, 0x702D39), `%d`, or string 11727 `Infinite` (proto flag 24h). Sets `[esp+3Ch]` = 1: the attack rows then start at StackLabel1. |
| Min block: AutoGather rate | 0x6991BF..0x699456 | confirmed, in game | As in "Gathering and the stat panel": `[panel+33Ch]`/`+340h`. `trickleDisplay` (WoL.dll) patches 0x69920E and 0x699227 inside it; `AddUnitPanelRow` hooks 0x699456. |
| Min block: attack rows | 0x699456..0x69A5D9 | confirmed, in game | 0x487EEE lists the unit's attack actions into 0xDCFD78 (count 0xDCFD7C); one **label-only** row per action into `[panel+344h + i*4]` (StackLabel0..2), `i < 3` (0x6994FF): `%s:  ` name, damage with type icon (`%d <icon="(10)…">`), range (`" %d <icon…>"`), bonus multipliers (`, %.1fx %s` / `, %dx %s`), help text as in the big panel. The special attacks (Critical Strike 64597, Lethal Strike 64603, Guardian 42873, Stomp 63679, Convert 64601) are gated by profile option 45h (0x699F76). |
| Bonus multiplier format | `, %.1fx %s` (wide) at 0xBA2060, the digit at 0xBA2068; `, %dx %s` at 0xC364A8 for a whole factor | byte search (2026-11-19), capstone | Pushed only at 0x4D7F7D (stat panel fill) and 0x699E1F (minimized block's attack rows), with the factor and the type's icon BString. The plain `%.1fx` at 0xBF1AD0 is pushed at 0x69BC28 (special attacks' rollover, string 18631 `Bonus Damage: %s`) and 0x6EEC97 (an ability's rollover). `multiplierDecimals` writes the digit; another DLL writes `2` there. |

The line goes into the row's help text through 0x4C5076(text, line, 1, 0), which puts `<ttf><ttb>` before it (0x4C50B0; `<ttf><ttr>` with the fourth argument 1, 0x4C8F35; `<ttf><tti>` with both 0). |
| Bonus type chain | fill: 0x4D7E86..0x4D7F1A and cold 0x69B7BA..0x69B968; block: 0x699A95..0x699D94 | capstone (2026-11-19), in game | ESI = the bonus's unittype, EAX = `[db+19B0h]`. Types with an icon: +47h, +14h, +4Eh, +56h, +2, +13h, +16h, +1, +55h, +49h, +7, +4Fh, +50h, +51h, +4Dh, +75h, +11h, +54h; any other is skipped (0x4D800B, 0x699F43). A branch formats `<icon=...>` into `[esp+28h]` (fill; `[esp+24h]` block), pushes the name's string ID and joins 0x4D7F44 / 0x699DE6. `attackTypeIcons` hooks the +11h test (0x69B909, 0x699D31). |
| Stat panel fill: rows | FUN_004D70ED, hot 0x4D70ED..0x4D82B4, cold 0x69A815..0x69C180 | confirmed (capstone), in game | Callers 0x4D6F7B and 0x9BBD5E (REA `ev_f1c7b337…`), both after 0x453B28 (selection), 0x4D7086 and 0x4D81B1 (hides the 9 rows through 0x448F13). Rows in order: **0** name (0x4702DC) / `%s (%s)` player and civ; **1** `Hitpoints:` / `%d/%d` or `%d/%d (%d+%d)`; then from row 2 (`[esp+2Ch]`): **Group Health** (0x69A9D3, when 0x4020C3(`[[0CF059Ch]]`) on 0xC66500 is true: a game config flag, name unknown); **resource held** (0x69AB0F, as the min block); **Gatherers** (0x69AE22, string 34986, `%d / %d`, single selection, `[unit+68h]` set and byte `[[proto+0E0h]+10h]` < FFh; meaning partly unknown); **AutoGather rate** (0x69AF6D); **Speed** (0x69B187: type `Unit` and the unit's vfunc +34h > 0; string 24323, `%.2f` or `%d`); **Resists** (0x69B34C: type `Unit` and `[proto+0C4h]` > 0, as the min block); one row per **attack** (0x4D78E7..0x4D8093: `%s:` name from `[db+1FE4h]`, value as the min block plus area and ScaleByContainedUnits `[action+48h]`); the **special attacks** (0x69BA53, profile option 45h). Unused rows hidden at 0x4D80F5..0x4D8169. The panel never reads ROF `[action+34h]`. |
| Profile option 45h | `optionenablerobustrollover` (the 70th `<Setting>` of the profile's GameOptions, as 49h is the 74th) | inferred (the profile's order) | Gates the special-attack rows of both panels. |
| `[screen+40Fh]` | 0x761BF6 | confirmed (capstone) | The option 49h copy WoL.dll's `forceMinUI` sets (0x761BF0). FUN_004C7E52 (0x4C8235) and FUN_004D70ED (0x4D721B) read the profile option itself, not this byte, and `forceMinUI` does not patch them. |
| Data-ref table | parser object (vtable 0xB9EA48, 18h bytes, one instance: `new` at 0x5D381B, ctor 0x5D31E3 at 0x5D3829, kept at `[owner+230h]`, REA `ev_3b54abf8…`) | confirmed (capstone) | Delimiters `{ ( } )` at `+4..+0Ah`; entries at `[+0Ch]`, count `[+10h]`, 16 bytes `{id, type, BString name, provider}`. The registration 0x5CFA7C (also vtable slot +2Ch, REA `ev_fcf05cc5…`) appends 116 entries; then vtable +28h = 0x5D3263 checks every `id == index` and type 1..4. Lookup by name 0x6326FF (linear, BString compare 0x4015C5); evaluators take `entries[id]`. So an entry appended with the next id works like a stock one. |
| Data refs by provider | 0x5CFA85..0x5D312A | confirmed (capstone) | Working: `currentUnitHP`/`Float` 0x6F4384, `MaximumHP` 0x6F44E3, `LOS` 0x6F46E2 (the getter 0x48206D: with modifiers), `Inv` 0x6F2B67, `Attack`/`AttackText`/`AttackFloat` 0x6F4776, `MaxVelocity`/`Float` 0x6F4831 (**the proto's base `[proto+80h]`**, not the live speed), `TrainPoints` 0x6F48B2, `BuildPoints` 0x6F4930, `BuildingWorkRate` 0x6F49B1, `Recharge` 0x6F462F, `CurrentAnimLength` 0x6F468A, `ID` 0x6F22F2, `OwnerID` 0x6F4A32, `Group` 0x6F4A88, `Name` 0x6F4F97, `ScenarioName` 0x6F502C, `PlayerIFF` 0x6F50A3, `InvList` 0x6F6216, `Capacity` 0x6F5931, `IFFcolor` 0x6F55DB, `LimberPercent` 0x6F5B80. **Stubs** (0x5FDE49, always fail): `AttackRange`, `MinAttackRange`, `BuildRate`, `GatherRate`, `AttackList`, `AttackString`, `GatherRateString` (the last made to work by `showGatherRate`). Ids: HP 0 … AttackText 9, AttackFloat 10 … LimberPercent 113, nativeTrainRegrow 115 (the last). |

### Stack portraits of the selection panel (WoL #67, mixed groups, 2026-11-17)

Read with capstone from the stock exe's bytes, then with REA (Ghidra 12.1.4, snapshot `age3y-b1a1dab7.json`, bundle
`rea/evidence/age3y-b1a1dab7/minui-stacks-20261117.json`; Ghidra splits these functions at the fill's `call next / jmp`
pairs). Seen in game the same day (WoL, 1920 x 1080; how-it-works, "minUIStats"). The selection panel object is the one
of "Unit panels" above. "Stack" = one group of the selection per proto.

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stack button bind | 0x9BAC34..0x9BAD00 (start of the selection panel bind) | confirmed (capstone) | `unitselectionpanel-unitStackButton%d` (0xC363D8), i = 0..8 (`cmp ebp,9` at 0x9BACC0), into the list `[panel+35Ch]` (count `+360h`, capacity `+364h`); `unitselectionpanel-unitStackButtonBig0` (0xC36388) into `[panel+368h]`; `[panel+36Ch]` = −1. `+320h` buttonArea, `+324h` StackButtonBigBackground, `+328h` stackQueue (row above). |
| Stacks | `[panel+280h]` list (count `[panel+284h]`), the current one `[panel+2BCh]` | confirmed (REA FUN_00460AB9, `ev_612415b0…`; in game) | Rebuilt from the selection: one stack per proto (`[unit+5Ch]`, 0x4C4EB0 finds or makes it), units of the first unit's owner only; **no cap** (12 types in game: 12 stacks). Tab / Shift+Tab (0x9BB2A0 / 0x9BB2C2) step `[panel+2BCh]` through all stacks modulo the count, then 0x9BB243 (0x4C939A) makes it current; a click on a portrait does the same (in game). The panel's name, portrait and rows describe the current stack's first unit (0x4C7DE2). |
| Hide all | 0x461059, `thiscall(panel)` | confirmed (capstone) | Hides (0x448F13) the 9 small buttons, Big0, the big background, the portrait, type name, player name, HP, resists, resource, count and its background, the tabs, the help button, the 3 health bars, the rate row and the 3 StackLabel/StackValue rows. |
| Stack fill | 0x4C940E (FUN_004C940E; Ghidra files the rest as FUN_004C94EF / FUN_004C7E52; one function, hot 0x4C940E..0x4C97F6, then 0x4C7DB3..0x4C83F5) | confirmed (REA `ev_8f615215…`, capstone; in game) | Calls the hide all, empties the list of placed buttons `[panel+2CCh]` (count `+2D0h`, cap `+2D4h`), sets `[panel+36Ch]` = −1. For i = 0 while **i < `[panel+360h]` (9) and i < `[panel+284h]`**: the stack's group, its first unit and proto; **Big0** `[panel+368h]` when i = `[panel+2BCh]` (then `[panel+36Ch]` = i), else the **small button i** `[[panel+35Ch]+i*4]` (cold 0x6985D6); its portrait (0x4C5702, vtable +104h), its count text `%d` (0xB9E478), help text, and its handle `[gadget+0Ch]` appended to the list (0x4C8441 at 0x4C97B0). Then `0x4C8F49(panel, &list)` at 0x4C7DB3, before the name and the minimized block. |
| Stack layout | 0x4C8F49..0x4C91EB, `thiscall(panel, list*)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (REA FUN_004C8F49, `ev_511b5dff…`, `ev_0fc23c71…`; in game) | Sum of the buttons' widths + 2 px per gap; x0 = buttonArea's left + (buttonArea's width − total) / 2, each button moved to (x, its own top) through vtable +2Ch (0x44B967) and **shown** (vtable +54h), x += width + 2. The button whose list index = `[panel+36Ch]` also gets the big background moved to (its left − 2, its top − 2) and shown. So the portraits are **centred** in buttonArea, not left-aligned at the declared 371. In game at 1920 x 1080: one type 885..945, five types 791..1039, nine types 697..1133 (pixels). |
| Minimized panel: icon size, health bars (2026-11-17) | 0xC36508 `%d <icon="(10)(TextureID(%d))">`, 0xC364C0 (range); stat panel 0xBA2100 `(16)` | confirmed (bytes; in game) | The minimized block's rows use `(10)` icons, the stat panel's `(16)`. The minimized block never shows `unitHealthBarGreen`/`Yellow`/`Red`: declared in the panel or in the portrait (as the big layout has them) they stay hidden in game; as children of `unitTypeName` (WoL's file, by its missing `/>`) they show with it, the right colour and length. |
| Gadget lookups | 0x40423C(handle, type mask): the manager's table `[mgr+68h]` (count `+6Ch`), handle & 3FFFFh, `[gadget+0Ch]` = handle, then vtable +0CCh tests the mask | confirmed (capstone; in game) | 0x404296 asks mask 0 (any gadget; the layout uses it), 0x447EEF mask 1, 0x44900A mask 10h (what 0x4C519C, and the DLL's text gadget access, use): a **button is not found with mask 10h**. Each adds a reference (`[gadget+5Ch]`), released by 0x40431B. |

Width = `[+40h] − [+30h]`. |

The hide does nothing when none of bits 0, 1, 7 is set. In game: the HP label reads as shown, the resource value of a unit holding nothing as not shown. |

The stat panel shows a range only with 0Ah. | |
| Action range by key | 0x4946D6, `float thiscall(action, key)`, `ret 4` | [O] capstone: the first entry of `[action+18h]` (16 bytes each, count `+1Ch`) whose `+0` equals key; its `+8` in ST0. Not found: a branch to 0x4B90CB (not read). With key −1 it gave 40 and 26 for the fort's attacks and 32 after `Revetment` (in game). | |

Referenced by the table entry 0xBA5270 only (no direct call). [O, in game] `<Flag>AlwaysDisplayRange</Flag>` on the Outpost reached the hook. | Site `ProtoFlagLastCompare`, `displayedRange`. |

The Explorer (Menelik II): 4 rows before the attacks, 5 of its 8 listed actions written. |
| Stat panel fill: attack row | 0x4D7A2E..0x4D7E0F | confirmed (REA `ev_cbfd5ca7…`, capstone; in game, compared row by row) | Label `%s:` (0xBA2140) with the string ID `[[db+1FE4h] + [action+4]*4]` (count `[db+1FDCh]`), or the raw name 0x5F8FC3 when negative. Damage `[[action+24h]]` × scale × the modifier record's `+8` × `+14h` (hand, ranged) or `+10h` (siege), each 1.0 below 1e-6 or without a record `[[unit+0B4h]+0C8h]`; scale = 1.0, or with flag 12h the contained units `[unit+74h]`, capped by `[action+48h]` when above 0; truncated, `%d <icon>` (0xBA2100) with the type's icon `[[game+920h]+1CCh + 4*type]`. With flag 0Ah, ` %d <icon>` (0xBA20B8): the max range of the range entry −1 (0x4946D6: entries of 10h at `[action+18h]`, count `+1Ch`, `+8` max) and the range icon `[[game+920h]+218h]`. Then the bonus multipliers `[action+28h]` (count `+2Ch`, 8-byte `{type, factor}`), `, %.1fx` with an icon per known type. |

**At 10 types or more** (in game, 10 and 12 types): the first nine stacks get a portrait, the others none; there is no
paging and no "+" button. The current type stays the big portrait when it is among the nine. The stock minimized layout
hides nothing else for a multi-type selection: the fill shows the portrait, type name, player name and help button
(0x4C836A..0x4C83DA) whatever the stack count, and the rows describe the current type. Not seen: Tab to a type past
the ninth (synthetic keys do not reach the game's hotkeys); by the code no portrait is then the big one.

| Rate getter / setter | 0x49EBB3 `float thiscall(action, key)`, `ret 4`; 0x5FA2FC `thiscall(action, key, float)`, `ret 8` | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | Exact key only. The getter returns 0 for no entry; the setter adds an entry when the rate is above 1e-6. |

Frame in applyData: action `[esp+20h]`, key `[esp+24h]`, amount `[esp+28h]`, relativity `[esp+2Ch]`, EDI target proto, ESI base proto, EBP 0. In unapplyData −4 each, ESI and EDI swapped. |
| Effect removal | FUN_0094DCB2 (a tech's effects, every entry) → FUN_007F8168 → vtable +28h | confirmed (`cs`), in game | Two callers: the TechStatus effect's general unapply 0x7F8D8E, and 0x8EA9FA. `trTechSetStatus` does not remove effects. At a scenario's start `Age0Egyptian`'s `WorkRate` on `All` (×0.8) is applied and removed for each player. |

| Projectile launch | 0x499422, `bool thiscall(unitAction, unit, target, [[frame+2CCh]+28h], byte, [ua+6Ch])`, `ret 14h` | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | Five arguments: the attack step pushes `[ebp+6Ch]` at 0x48F83B, then the byte of tactics flag 18h, `[[esp+2CCh]+28h]`, the target EDI and the unit `[ebp+10h]` (0x48F87D..0x48F88A); returns at 0x4999DA / 0x6A4C65 (`ret 14h`). The broadside step passes `[esi+94h]`, 0, 0, EBX, `[esi+10h]` (0x4CEA3A). **[O]** Called 4 times in a row for one shot (`numberOfShots projectiles`, 2026-11-12): 4 tracers, 4 hits. |

With profile option 57h (`[esp+28h]`) and BL, every unit with proto flag 5 queues its HP bar (0x511D80). **[O]** A posted `WM_KEYDOWN` of `VK_MENU` sets it (2026-11-12). |
| Attack wait | 0x48FA69, `thiscall(actionManager, int ms)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | `[mgr+0B8h]` = now `[world+108h]`, `[mgr+0BCh]` = ms × the unit's ROF modifier (`[[[unit+0B4h]+0C8h]+34h]`, 1.0 without a record; rounded down). `rofFix` keeps orders from clearing `[+0B8h]`. Seven callers, all in the attack class: 0x48A80C, 0x48A8A3, 0x48E9F0 (the override ROF), 0x48A833, 0x48A9AC, 0x48EA1E (tactics ROF after an attack that did not fire; tactics flag bit 15 decides, 0x4A8539 / 0x4A8680 / 0x4B338F), 0x48F8E9 (after a shot). **[O]** In game only 0x4A1189 and 0x48F8B5 read the ROF while a Blockhouse fought; the three others were not seen. |

### Building work rate auras (for `buildingWorkRateAuras`)

The getter 0x9BFE33 and its four callers are in "Building work rate"; the modifier action, its
creation 0x4DD22D, the action lists, the collector and the sum 0x4B3C7D in "Snare and group
movement".

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| ModifyType name → stat | FUN_005F5645, `int cdecl(BString*)` | tail and callers confirmed; name table scoping | Compares through 0x443C10 (case ignored); Speed 0, MaxHP 1, Damage 2, MovingDamage 3, Bounty 4, SiegeDamage 5, UnitDamage 6, LOS 7, Armor 8, GatherRate 0Bh, AbilityROF 0Ch, ROF 0Dh, AutoGatherRate 0Eh, else −1 (the cold returns of 3, 7, 8, 0Ch and 0Dh at 0x67D730..0x67D752 confirmed). Tail 0x5FE6C7..0x5FE6E1: `neg eax / sbb eax,eax / and al,0F1h / pop esi / add eax,0Eh / ret` (site `ModifyTypeNameTail`). ESI = the name throughout. With `buildingWorkRateAuras`, `BuildingWorkRate` → 40h (in game). |
| Stat sum | FUN_004B3C7D, `bool cdecl(float* value, list, int stat)` | confirmed (static, 2026-11-11; the add path in game) | Walks the unit's type-2Ch modifiers in list order (oldest first): `[+78h]` 0 sets `[+68h]`, 1 adds it, 2 multiplies; stops after the first with flag 16h. Callers: 0x4B3C48 Speed from `[proto+80h]`, 0x4B3A0E MaxHP from `[proto+98h]`; 0x4B3A41 (2), 0x4B3A6D (3), 0x4B3AF1 (4), 0x4B3A99 (5), 0x4B3AC5 (6), 0x4B3B1D (7), 0x4B3B49 (8), 0x4B3B75 (0Bh), 0x4B3C1C (0Dh), 0x4B3BA1 (0Eh) from 1.0. The init 0x4B3D44 stores stat `[+64h]`, value `[+68h]`, operation `[+78h]` unchecked. In game (`veterancyBonus` op add): Damage ×1.5, +0.5, ×4/3 gave ×2.667; MaxHP +100 raised 352 to 452. Evidence: `rea/evidence/age3y-b1a1dab7/veterancy-add-op.json` (ev_8f89bc2e…, ev_c42b6079…, ev_4c2204d2…, ev_3096f165…). |
| ModifyType callers | 0x5FC8AE (tactics tag, dispatch 0x5F9FD0); 0x5F3EFA (powers `<unitModify>`, FUN_005F3E97) | confirmed (call sites); rest scoping | Tactics: `mov [ebp+0B8h],al` with no check (default 0FFh at 0x5FA4E7). Powers: `mov ebp,eax / test ebp,ebp / jl` → error 1233h; stores {stat, op, amount} in `[obj+128h]`, applied by 0x8EE18E → 0x4DD22D. |
| Tactics ModifyType byte | `[tacticAction+B8h]` (signed byte) | scoping | Read by 0x9D064C (aura apply), 0x9D7696 (second applier), 0x9E3727 (init of class 0xB97C78) and 0x9D6F40 (aura tooltip: `stat − 1` ≤ 5, else default). `<ModifyMultiplier>` → `[+ACh]`, `<ModifyProtoID>` → `[+9Eh]`, `<ModifyAbstractType>` → `[+9Ch]`. |
| Aura apply | FUN_009D033E, call 0x4DD22D at 0x9D0670; filter 0x9D0238; scan 0x9CFF9A → 0x4A1C55 ("Resource auras", below in this file's order, has their signatures) | confirmed for a completed building, in game | `0x4DD22D(target, stat, mult, −1f, −1f, 0, 2, 0 ms, −1, −1, exclusive)`. Filter: not the owner, same player unless tactic flag 20h, alive, complete (0x44DDB4), ModifyProtoID / ModifyAbstractType. Runs only when the multiplier is > 1e-6 and the owner is complete. Keeps {target ID, modifier ID} at `[ua+68h]`. In game, an Explorer's aura reached a completed BloodFactory. |
| Aura update's steps | FUN_009D033E | confirmed (REA `ev_7ffb6afa…`), in game | Each update scans, keeps the targets in `[ua+68h]` still in the scan, removes the modifier of those that left (0x9C05B8), and sends each new target through the filter 0x9D0238 (one call, 0x9D0633) to 0x4DD22D. A target the filter refused is not kept, so every update asks again. The modifier of a dead carrier is gone at once (20.06 s; the next carrier's at 20.67 s, in game). |
| Modifier fields | `+64h` stat, `+68h` value, `+6Ch`..`+74h` ramp, `+78h` op, `+7Ch`/`+80h` the two −1 arguments, `+84h` −1, `+88h` duration; saved by 0x9E3423, loaded by 0x9E355E | confirmed (REA `ev_fad68dd5…`, `ev_726c449d…`), in game | `+84h` is read by nothing but the save (`auraStacking` kept a mark there until 2026-11-21). Nothing on a modifier says which aura made it: `[+4]` is null on the 0x4DD22D path. `<ModifyExclusive>` is action flag 21h, passed as the last argument and stored as state bit 16h. |
| Second aura applier | FUN_009D7328 (`[ua+6Ch]`, no range, no filter); one caller, 0x9D7FFF, which is not inside a Ghidra function | confirmed (REA `ev_76e6d312…`) | Applies a modifier to every unit of a type `[action+9Ch]`/`[+9Eh]` of the player, with no range, with the same steps as the aura update and a query by proto or abstract type; a proto with flag 52h takes the value from the dancers. Call of 0x4DD22D after 0x9D769D. `auraStacking` does not cover it. (An earlier scoping pass found no direct caller and took it for a vtable slot: Ghidra had no function around the call 0x9D7FFF.) |
| Modifier action classes | vtable 0xB97C08 (ctor 0x4DDA01, from 0x4B3D9E); vtable 0xB97C78 (ctor 0x9E36CC, factory case 46h) | scoping | Both carry runtime type 2Ch. WoL's ModifyActionFix retypes the second to 46h (0x9E36D0) and replaces the collector call 0x49F251. |
| Collector call | 0x49F23C..0x49F251 in FUN_0049F1BA | confirmed | Clears the count `[0CF071Ch]`, then `mov ecx,[edi+58h] / push 0CF0718h / push 1 / push 2Ch / add ecx,14h / call 49F1A0h`; then per-stat helpers from 0x49F26A. |
| GatherRate helper | 0x4B3B75 | confirmed | `[esp] = 1.0f`, `0x4B3C7D(&v, list, 0Bh)`; stores v only when it matched (cold 0x6A8972). The model for stat 40h. |
| GatherRate / AutoGatherRate readers | 0x4ECFE9 → 0x69FE53 (`+24h`); 0x49EDB8 → 0x69FE87 (`+28h`; another `+28h` reader at 0x69FE6F) | confirmed (0x4ECFE9, 0x69FE53, 0x49EDB8, 0x69FE6F); 0x69FE87 scoping | Record `[[unit+B4h]+C8h]` (no null check of `[unit+B4h]`); field used when `fcomp` with 0xB9FB64 (1e-6f) is not below and ordered-or-NaN (`test ah,5 / jnp`), else 1.0 (0xB856BC). Sites `BwrGatherRateRecord`, `BwrGatherRateRule`. |

The pass after a load 0x94DDEA has one caller, 0x94CCB5, and calls 0x7F7E97(player, unit, techId, **1**, 0) at 0x94DF10 for flag-20h effects only | confirmed (REA `ev_093575dc…`, `ev_1fbf0dc8…`, `ev_ebcbf644…`; `cs`) | A byte scan of `.text` finds no stock write of bit 20h into `[effect+9]` but the constructors' `mov byte [eax+9],28h` (kind 8 "general") and two `3Ah` at 0xA23BEF / 0xA23C77; `or byte [eax+9],20h` at 0x5194EF is a proto flag (`[proto+50h]`). So a stock Data effect is not in that pass. **The load runs them another way (seen in game, 2026-11-25, `nurseryFactor log`):** after `loadGame`, every ProtoUnit Data effect of the active techs came through the applyData switch again (our subtypes' handlers, `researchGrants`' `ResearchGrantMax` too), before the first world tick, in the order of the techs in the techtree, not the order they were researched (`NurseryFactorMax` +5 of a later tech then `Assign` 4 of an earlier-researched one: cap 4 after the load, 9 before). A Player-target subtype (`FreeHomeCityUnitByNurseryFactor`) did not run again. The caller of that replay is not identified yet. |

The bool is the action's flag bit 6; offset the zero vector 0xCF0618. (2026-11-21, REA `ev_71ba0bc4…`, `ev_32be21bf…`) The units come from the box query 0x462E95 (up to 1000), sorted by 0x4CA5D4 when `[action+7Ch]` > 0 (by distance or along the offset: no drawing); each one goes to TakeDamage 0x49B3D9(player, proto, action, attacker, multiplier, 0, cap, 0, **the bool**, 1, 1, **−2**, 1). In TakeDamage the bool is read once: when it is 0, the sound manager `[world+950h]` plays the hit sound (0x484136 type 3, the attacker proto's `[+F4h]`), by chance the pain sound (type 2) and 0x49BC87; −2 skips the impact 0x49AA03; the flinch 0x49C0D4 (anim 23h) plays either way. So the area damage draws no effect on its victims. |

### KB unit queries (for `kbUnitSyscalls`, 2026-11-03)

The stock KB syscalls share one head (for example `kbUnitGetNumberWorkers` 0x881CC6, registered
at 0x8879E6 with `push 2 / push fn / push name / mov ecx,esi / call 624F52h`): world
`[[C66234h]+13Ch]` non-null, player `0x44CB21([C66664h])`, its kb `[player+80h]`, then a kb method
with the unit ID. Player by ID and Unit by ID are in "Home city gather point"; the inventory and
ResList get in "Resource inventories".

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| kb getNumberWorkers | 0x86C647, `thiscall(kb, int unitId)` | confirmed (`cs`) | Returns `[unit+98h]` only when the unit's owner `[[unit+44h]]` is the kb's player `[kb+10h]`; else 0. |
| Unit worker array | `[unit+94h]` (pointer), `[unit+98h]` (count) | confirmed (0x86C647); entry layout inference | 18h-byte entries whose first dword is a worker's unit ID (the layout another DLL's code walks; not traced in the exe). |
| kb unit record | 0x470CC4, `thiscall(kb, int unitId)` | confirmed (`cs`) | `[kb+18h][[[unit+B4h]+8]]` if that index is below `[kb+1Ch]`: the AI's record of the unit, null if it never knew it. |
| `kbUnitVisible` | 0x88596E (registered at 0x88E79C) | confirmed (`cs`) | True when `[[unit+B4h]+ACh] & (1 << [C66664h])`: the per-player visibility mask. |
| Unit isSeeable | 0x48A9EF, `bool thiscall(unit, int player)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (head, `cs`); cold path scoping | The visibility mask above; else (0x55F5B2) the explored mask `[+B0h]` and the proto's under-fog flags `[[proto+50h]+1] & 1` (proto `[unit+5Ch]`). Site `UnitIsSeeable`. |
| Unit getSquad | 0x4E2A03, `thiscall(unit)` | confirmed (`cs`) | `[[world+148h]][[unit+B4h]+14h]` if that index is below `[world+14Ch]`, else 0 (0x470CB5). Site `UnitGetSquad`. |
| Squad orders | `[squad+1Ch]` → `+28h` pointer array, `+2Ch` count; each element `+1Ch` array of 18h-byte entries, `+20h` count | inference | The path `kbUnitGetNumberTargeters` walks; the first dword of an entry is a target unit ID. Seen in another DLL's code, not traced in the exe. |
| Resource inventory isFull | 0x4DC520, `bool thiscall(inventory)` | head confirmed | Inventory `[[unit+B4h]+94h]`; starts with the unit lookup of its `+0` unit ID. Site `InventoryIsFull`. |
| Unit getCarryCapacity | 0x494503, `float thiscall(unit, int r)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (`cs`) | `0x4944E5([unit+5Ch], r)`: the proto's capacity. Site `UnitCarryCapacity`. |

### Auto scout: move orders, the explored grid, tactics and the leash (for `autoScout`, WoL #68, 2026-11-19)

Evidence: REA `ev_3bf8e27c…` (string `trUnitMoveToPoint`), `ev_ff146c71…` (0x833532), `ev_d2636748…` (0x568A1A),
`ev_656adcf2…` (the `Wander` strings), `ev_157df32d…` (`BUGA::doWander` 0x4E35B9), `ev_7f30199c…` (0x4E33BE),
`ev_d400a252…` (0x4E3A37), `ev_c497050e…` (0x7C8E49), `ev_b587828d…` (0x93DF50), `ev_f4e16166…` (0x50E36A),
`ev_1b777940…` (`kbCanPath2`); bundle `rea/evidence/age3y-b1a1dab7-autoscout.json`, snapshot `age3y-b1a1dab7.json`. The
rest by capstone (`cs`), memory reads and a read breakpoint (`trace_writes.py --rw` on the leash anchor), in game
(Romanian ravens, a Dutch envoy and a caravel on Great Lakes, 2026-11-19).

| Name | Address | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trUnitMoveToPoint` | 0x98674C (registered at 0x98940E) | confirmed (`cs`) | Copies the trigger's selected units and the point, then 0x833532: a **network command**, not a simulation call. |
| Move command sender | 0x833532, `thiscall(game, player, ...)` | confirmed (REA) | 0x5695FC(player, 0, 0, -1) (type 0, the work command: `new(0A4h)`, ctor 0x7D4200, vtable 0xB95588), 0x5683B9, `[cmd+2Ch]=3`, `[cmd+3Ch]=0`, 0x5696E1 (units), 0x7C75A9 (waypoints), flags in `[[cmd+58h]]`, then 0x568A1A, the command queue (it ends in 0x7C8F0A, which hands it to the network object, vtable `+94h`). Simulation code must not use it. |
| Command execute | FUN_007C8E49, `thiscall(cmd)`; one call, 0x64D31A, in the world's command processing 0x490015 (called at 0x481BC2 by the world update) | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | Every command runs here, inside the world update, on every machine. `[cmd+81h]` type byte (0 work: move, attack, gather, garrison; 9 stop, 0x832D37; 19h set tactic or stance, 0x82FC85), `[cmd+20h]` player, `[cmd+40h]`/`[cmd+44h]` unit IDs and count, `[cmd+3Ch]` 0 for unit commands. `[player+3C4h]` counts them. Then 0x7C87FF (group commands, slot 58h: the work command's 0x7D42B6) or 0x7C86A4 (per unit, slot 54h: the set-tactic command's 0x7D107C). Head `push ecx / push esi / mov esi,ecx / cmp dword [esi+5Ch],5`, nothing branches inside (`cs`). Site `AutoScoutCmdExecute` (`autoScout`). |
| Action manager doMove | 0x50E36A, `bool thiscall(mgr = [unit+58h], Vec3* point, float tolerance, char a3, char a4, int orderId, char a6, char a7, int a8, int a9, float a10)`, `ret 28h` | confirmed (REA, in game) | Refuses a proto with flag byte `[[proto+50h]]` bit 4. A Move action: 0x48D927(world, 8), 0x48D9CB(unit ID, 0), `[act+24h]` = tolerance, `[act+68h]` = a10; a3 sets `[act+64h]`, `[act+80h]` and 0x9E3E8C; **a4 = 1 sets action flag 17h**, which the leash honours (below); a7 sets flag 1Dh; 0x48DBEE(orderId) (-1: none; another value must be a live order of `[world+8]`, 0x7C9179, or it falls back to -1); 0x4BA4FF(point, 1) the waypoint; 0x494894(mgr, action, 1, 0, a6) adds it; 0x47154B frees it on failure. 9 callers, among them the unit order code 0x9444D3 (`(pt, 0.1f, 0, 0, order, 0, 0, -1, -1, -1.0f)`, after 0x9C9E54, which only writes `[mgr+94h] = -1`) and the leash 0x50E50E. In game an air, a land and a water unit went where they were sent. Site `AutoScoutDoMove`. |
| Flee from a unit | FUN_004E3A37, `thiscall(mgr, unitId, ...)`, `ret 0Ch` | REA | Builds a point away from the unit with the synced RNG (seed table 0xC66588, stream 2), a Move action (flag 18h), 0x4BA4FF, 0x494894. The same building blocks as doMove. |
| Create child move action | 0x4E33BE, `thiscall(parentAction, unit, targetUnit, Vec3*, float, float, float, float, char)` | REA | For an action's own child moves (the animal AI's wander, 0x4E3905). Not a top-level order. |
| Waypoints of a move | 0x4BA4FF `bool thiscall(action, Vec3* points, int count)`, `ret 8`; 0x4E3562 appends one | confirmed (`cs`) | `[act+4Ch]` points (12 B each), `[act+50h]` count. |
| Leash | 0x489B15, `thiscall(mgr)`, called by the action manager update (0x48981F) | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | Returns at once without a current action `[mgr+24h]`, for an order ID `[act+0Ch]` ≥ 0, for action types 3, 6, 2Fh, 3Ah, 9, 2Eh, 2Dh, 1Eh, for a **Move with flag 17h** (0x4B95E4), and for a herded unit (`[mgr+ACh]` ≥ 0). Else it compares the distance to the anchor `[mgr+7Ch]` (x, y, z; read every frame at 0x489C43) with a range (0x48CA54) and, past it, walks the unit back: 0x50E50E, doMove(anchor, 1.0f, …). A raven moved by doMove without flag 17h was walked back to its anchor every 7 s in game. |
| Anchor | `[mgr+7Ch]`, `[mgr+80h]`, `[mgr+84h]`; another copy at `[mgr+70h]` | in game | A scenario's raven kept (55.0, 59.1) for minutes; no write during 90 s of moves (`trace_writes.py`). `autoScout` keeps a scout's anchor on its position (sim state, the same write on every machine). Which stock code writes it was not traced. |
| Current tactic | `[mgr+0A8h]`, an index into `[[proto+44h]+10h]` (count `+14h`) | confirmed | A tactic's `+8` is its name ID: an index into the database's tactic names `[db+1FF0h]` (one pointer to UTF-16 text each, count `+1FF4h`; lookup 0x5E0CA6). In game, 95 names in WoL, `AutoScout` = 17. A unit is born in its first tactic (index 0, in game). |
| Tactic index by name | 0x948161, `int thiscall(actionSet = [proto+44h], int nameId)`, `ret 4` | confirmed (`cs`) | -1 when the proto has no such tactic. Site `AutoScoutTacticIndex`. |
| setTactic | 0x4F7ECD, `thiscall(mgr, int player, int index, -1, 1, -1.0f, 1, 0)`, `ret 1Ch` | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | What the set-tactic command runs per unit (0x7D107C: owner check, 0x48042E(mgr, 0), 0x948161, then this with `[cmd+24h]`, the player). Refuses a tactic whose age `[tactic+194h]` the player has not reached. Site `AutoScoutSetTactic`. |
| `unitSetTactic("Name")` | UI command 0x7DD64D (registered at 0x53A1BA) | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | Looks the name up (0x5E0CA6) and sends a set-tactic command (type 19h, 0x82FC85; `[cmd+90h]` the name ID) for the selection. A button's `<command>`; it travels like any command and the tactic is kept in saved games. |
| Transition tags | the tactics parser FUN_005F9763, its transition part from 0x5FC0E4 (`Tactic` 0xB9F41C, `Action` 0xBCA174, `Length` 0xBCACB4, `Exit` 0xBA3284, `Enter` 0xBCACFC, `Automatic` 0xBCACE8, `CommandAutomatic` 0xBCACC4, `LandOnly` 0xBCAD10) | confirmed (static, `cs`) | A `<transition>` is 1Ch bytes in the tactic's list (`+20h`, count `+24h`): `+0` the target tactic's index, `+8` what it plays, `+0Ch`/`+10h` a bit set: `<exit>1` bit 1, `<automatic>1` bit 2 (`or` at 0x5FC3BC); `<enter>` bit 0 and `<commandautomatic>` bit 3 inferred (0x4F7E64 reads bit 0 for its "enter" case, 0x423998(3) bit 3). setTactic 0x4F7ECD asks the current tactic for an exit transition to the target (0x4F7E64(index, 0): bit 1) and, when its `+8` is set, plays it for `<length>` (0x4F7C58); else it switches at once (0x4F7389). Read from the game (#84, a WoL copy): a parsed transition holds `+0` target 3, `+4` 2Ah, `+8` 7D0h (the `<length>` in ms, so `+8` is the length and 0x4F7C58 makes an action of type 2Ah with it), bit set 02h. The stock artillery's transitions all name `<action>Limber</action>`, which no tactics file defines (a built-in, inferred); the dismount pack names it too, and with it the dismount and mount played in game (whether the transition plays without it was not measured). Adding new names to `data\animtypes.xml` made the unit play its first tactic's attack while on foot: leave that list alone. Evidence `r1121-g-dismount-tactics.json`, `r1121-g-dismount-transitions.json`. |
| Tactic for an action | FUN_004B2CE6, `thiscall(actionSet, unit, bool command, char allowSwitch, action)` | confirmed (REA, static) | The current tactic `[mgr+0A8h]` when it holds the action (0x4B2C30); else, with allowSwitch, the target of the first transition of the current tactic with bit 2 (`<automatic>`), or bit 3 on a command, whose tactic holds it (and the age `[tactic+10h]`); else -1. Its caller FUN_004B2F4B (adding an action to a unit) then switches with 0x4F7ECD. So a tactic whose transitions have no `<automatic>` is never left for an action: measured in game (#84), a dismounted stance kept index 3 through a fight. |
| Squad mode of a unit | `[mgr+0CCh]` (byte, 0FFh none); setter 0x9CE889 `thiscall(mgr, player, char mode, char setTactic, int, char force)` | confirmed (REA, static) | Checks the mode against the unit (0x93E714) and the proto's tactic for the mode's tactic name (`[mode+28h]`, 0x948161), stores the mode, and with setTactic switches to that tactic (0x4F7ECD); mode -1 puts the unit back in its first tactic. One direct caller, the army's member loop 0x4F9E05 (`0x6AA3FE`), when a member's mode or tactic differ from the army's (0x7AA5E3) and a distance test (0x4FA8F8 over 50, 30 for some units) passes. In game (#84) it never ran on four dismounted chasseurs in a skirmish fight (a hook at 0x6AA3D7 was never reached). |
| `setSquadMode("Name")` | UI command 0x7DE808 (registered at 0x53A30A) | static; in game it did nothing from the console | Looks the mode up by name in `[player+580h]` (0x7AA5A4), checks each selected unit (owner, 0x93E714, the tactic 0x948161) and sends a squad-mode command per proto (0x7DE67F -> 0x82FD72). From the console (#84, skirmish) four chasseurs did not change tactic; not traced further. |
| Explored grid | `[world+250h]`: width `+14h` (x), height `+18h` (z), cells `+20h`, one dword each, index x × height + z | confirmed (`cs`, in game) | A cell is `floor(coord × [[world+88h]+1Ch])` (2 m cells: 0.5 per metre in game). The player's mask is `[[world+2D8h]+4·id]` (count `[world+2DCh]`): `cell & mask & FFFFh` = explored, `& FFFF0000h` = visible now (0x44E9FE: 0 unexplored, 1 visible, 2 explored). Point lookup 0x44E921 `thiscall(player, Vec3*)`; 0x4601B8 adds the display switch `[world+6922h]`, which console `blackmap` toggles (0x7D96EE): the grid itself is simulation state. `testbed.py explored` reads it. |
| Water at a tile | terrain `[world+88h]` vtable `+194h` = 0x4CB5B2, `byte thiscall(terrain, x, z)`, `ret 8` | confirmed (`cs`) | The water type of the tile's index in the byte grid `[terrain+9Ch]` (row length `[terrain+0Ch]`), through `[[game+91Ch]+540h]`; 0FFh for land or out of range. Same tiles as the visibility cells. |
| Movement type | `[proto+1D2h]` byte, from `<MovementType>` (0x5F5A74) | confirmed (`cs`) | 1 land, 2 water, 4 air. |
| `kbCanPath2` | 0x8827CC | REA | Goes through the AI knowledge base of the AI player `[0xC66664]` (`[player+80h]`, 0x87157B): not usable from simulation code for every player. |
| Animal wander | `BUGA::doWander` 0x4E35B9 (from 0x4E35AA) | REA | Only for a herded unit: the herd of `[mgr+ACh]` in the herd manager `[world+C0h]` (centre `[herd+4]`/`+0Ch`, radius `[herd+20h]`), a child move every 120 s. Cfg `noWander` at 0xD98F88 (every animal). `<WanderDistance>` is `[proto+C8h]` (0x5FC783); `<Flag>Wanders</Flag>` is bit 0 of `[[proto+50h]+4]`. WoL's raven has no herd (`[mgr+ACh]` = -1 in game): its circle came from the leash, not from the wander. |

function at the action's methods, so they were read with capstone (`rea/evidence/wol-20261120-G/truckattack_cs.txt`).
In game (WoL copy, 2026-11-20): the action list of an enemy `MonsterTruckA`, and the inventory of Mills read from
memory (`inv.py` in the same folder).

| Name | Address | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

| Undo manager | `[game+928h]`; undo 0x9BE4E2, redo 0x9BE535 (`thiscall(um)`) | confirmed (`cs`), layout inferred | [I] Undo entries `{[um+0] data, [um+4] count}`, redo entries `{[um+0Ch], [um+10h]}` (0x9BE4E5, 0x9BE4EE), `[um+18h]` set while undoing. |
| Game vtable | 0xB88628 (ctor 0x55AF45, `mov [esi],0B88628h` at 0x55AF64; the object is made at 0x5AB384 and stored in 0xC66234 at 0x5AB3A2) | confirmed (`cs`) | +5Ch the chat (trChatSend), +0C0h the scenario save. |
| Scenario save | vtable +0C0h = 0x703ED3 (`jmp 834583h`) → FUN_00834583 `thiscall(game, dir, BString* name, char flag)`, `ret 0Ch`, returns true when written (`ev_8622922c…`) | confirmed (REA, `cs`, in game) | Returns false while `[game+0AB0h]` (a save running) is set, or for an empty or invalid name (0x401F47, 0xA461A7). Shows the "Saving" dialog (0x83A2B8 with `[game+920h]`), **sets the open scenario's name** `BStringSet([game+958h], name)` at 0x8345E5 and `[game+95Ch]` = flag at 0x8345FD, offers to overwrite a read-only file (string 4BAEh), copies an existing file to `.bak` (0x8346C3..), sets `[game+0AB0h]` around the write, writes through FUN_00833DDC (`ev_2ac8c3da…`: free space ≥ 20 MB, a `.tmp` then the file) and hides the dialog. [O] After `saveScenario("autosave-1")` the editor's Save (`uiSaveScenarioBrowser`) wrote `autosave-1` (no dialog), and made `autosave-1.bak`. |
| Open scenario's name | BString `[game+958h]` | confirmed (in game) | [O] `e2-base` after saving as it, `autosave-1` after `saveScenario("autosave-1")`; the text emptied (length 0) when a new editor opens. The editor's Save writes to it without a dialog when it is set. |
| Recent scenarios | FUN_008338F4 `thiscall(game, BString name, uimgr+180h)` (`ev_e25c9a4d…`) | confirmed (REA) | Returns for a name starting with `~` (as `~last`, `~testing`); else puts it first in `[game+93Ch]` (count `[game+940h]`, at most 10) and runs `uiRefreshEditorMenu`. Only the console handler calls it after a save, so a direct call of the save leaves the list alone. |
| "Modified" flag | none found | unknown | [U] The save and its writer set no flag the editor reads back; a bounded look, not a proof. `editorAutosave` uses the undo manager and the last input instead. |

### Fonts and text glyphs (WoL request: missing letters ă ș ț, 2026-11-18)

Static reading of the exe and the files (addresses not re-checked with REA for this note: `unverified`); the behaviour
was then checked in game in `C:\AoE3-UHC-WoLReal5` (Wars of Liberty, our `UHC.dll`), with test strings in the string
table: [wol-fixes/fonts/README.txt](../wol-fixes/fonts/README.txt).<!-- private -->

| What | Where | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Font loader | 0x545660 | unverified (address); confirmed in game (behaviour) | Opens `FONTS\fonts.bar`, enumerates `*.ttf` and hands each file to `AddFontMemResourceEx` (call at 0x545954), so the fonts are private to the process. **Loose `.ttf` files in `FONTS\` are loaded too** (confirmed in game: three fonts with new family names, not installed in Windows and not in any archive, drew the main menu, tooltips and titles). Inferred: the enumeration goes through the game's file layer, which lists loose files beside the archive's. |
| Font table | `FONTS\fonts3.xml` (UTF-16LE with BOM) | confirmed in game | `<Font Name>` entries, `<param TypeFace=…>` (a GDI family name), `Bold`, `AscentHeight`, `Antialiased`, `ResolutionOverride`. A TypeFace changed to a new family name is honoured; the rest of the entry is unchanged (`Bold` is synthesised by GDI). |
| Fallback font | pointer 0xD90774 → string 0xBE1DF4 `Arial Unicode MS` | unverified (address); confirmed in game (behaviour) | For every font the engine also makes "<name> - Fallback" with this TypeFace. A character the primary font lacks is drawn from it: ă in Formal436 BT text showed in Arial Unicode's style. |
| Coverage map | `GetFontUnicodeRanges` call 0x54B6A2 | unverified | A 65,536-bit map per font, from the font's cmap (not from the OS/2 range bits). |
| Per-character pick | 0x404CFB | unverified (address); confirmed in game (behaviour) | Primary font if its map has the character, else the fallback, else `?` (0x448356): ș and ț (U+0219, U+021B), absent from Arial Unicode MS too, showed as `?`. |
| Glyph source | `GetGlyphOutlineW`, DEFAULT_CHARSET | unverified | No code-point limit was seen: U+0100–U+017F and U+0218–U+021B drew once the fonts had them. |

Not a font problem: text with accents inside an XS string literal fails because the XS tokenizer cuts a string at any
byte above 0x7F ([wol-maps.md](wol-maps.md), lines 58–95).

Three cultures only: there is no slot for a fourth. |
